00030: def B(a, name, size, center, color):
00031:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00032:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00033:     a.include(name, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))

00035: def R(a, name, radius, start, end, color):
00036:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00037:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (*start, *end))
00038:     assert sum((u-v)**2 for u,v in zip(start,end)) > 1e-16
00039:     a.include(name, rod(radius=radius, start=start, end=end, color=color))

00212: def textured_material(mat,kind):
00213:     mat.use_nodes=True
00214:     nodes=mat.node_tree.nodes
00215:     links=mat.node_tree.links
00216:     bsdf=nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00217:     if bsdf is None:
00218:         return
00219:     base=tuple(bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].default_value)
00220:     coord=nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00221:     mapping=nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00222:     mapping.operation='MULTIPLY'
00223:     mapping.inputs[1].default_value=(24,1.4,5) if kind=="wood" else (1,1,1)
00224:     tex=nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00225:     tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value=5 if kind=="wood" else 7
00226:     tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value=3
00227:     links.new(coord.outputs["Generated"],mapping.inputs[0])
00228:     links.new(mapping.outputs[0],tex.inputs["Vector"])
00229:     ramp=nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00230:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position=.18
00231:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position=.82
00232:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color=tuple(v*.55 for v in base[:3])+(1,)
00233:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color=tuple(min(v*1.15,1) for v in base[:3])+(1,)
00234:     links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],ramp.inputs[0])
00235:     links.new(ramp.outputs[0],bsdf.inputs["Base Color"])
00236:     bump=nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00237:     bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value=.19
00238:     bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value=.025 if kind=="wood" else .018
00239:     links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00240:     links.new(bump.outputs[0],bsdf.inputs["Normal"])
00241:     bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value=.76 if kind=="wood" else .87
00242:

00237: # Procedural surface variation; no image textures.
00238: def textured_material(mat, kind):
00239:     mat.use_nodes=True
00240:     nodes=mat.node_tree.nodes
00241:     links=mat.node_tree.links
00242:     bsdf=nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00243:     if not bsdf:
00244:         return
00245:     base=tuple(bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].default_value)
00246:     tex=nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00247:     tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value=5 if kind=="wood" else 7
00248:     tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value=3
00249:     coord=nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00250:     mapping=nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00251:     mapping.operation='MULTIPLY'
00252:     mapping.inputs[1].default_value=(24,1.4,5) if kind=="wood" else (1,1,1)
00253:     links.new(coord.outputs["Generated"],mapping.inputs[0])
00254:     links.new(mapping.outputs[0],tex.inputs["Vector"])
00255:     ramp=nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00256:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position=.18
00257:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position=.82
00258:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color=tuple(v*.72 for v in base[:3])+(1,)
00259:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color=tuple(min(v*1.13,1) for v in base[:3])+(1,)
00260:     links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],ramp.inputs[0])
00261:     links.new(ramp.outputs[0],bsdf.inputs["Base Color"])
00262:     bump=nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00263:     bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value=.19
00264:     bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value=.035 if kind=="wood" else .018
00265:     links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00266:     links.new(bump.outputs[0],bsdf.inputs["Normal"])
00267:     bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value=.83
00268: 
00269: # Finish classification uses the actual material color, independent of mesh names.
00270: for mat in list(bpy.data.materials):
00271:     mat.use_nodes=True
00272:     bsdf=mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00273:     if not bsdf:
00274:         continue
00275:     r,g,b,_=bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].default_value
00276:     if r > g*1.20 and g > b*1.18:
00277:         textured_material(mat,"wood")
00278:     elif .30 < r < .77 and .29 < g < .78 and b < .60:
00279:         textured_material(mat,"plaster")
00280:     elif max(r,g,b)<.19:
00281:         bsdf.inputs["Metallic"].default_value=.55
00282:         bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value=.36
00283:     else:
00284:         bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value=.84

00289: def textured_material(mat, kind):
00290:     mat.use_nodes = True
00291:     nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes
00292:     links = mat.node_tree.links
00293:     bsdf = nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00294:     if bsdf is None:
00295:         return
00296:     base = tuple(bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].default_value)
00297:     tex = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00298:     tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 5 if kind == "wood" else 7
00299:     tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 3
00300:     coord = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00301:     mapping = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00302:     mapping.operation = 'MULTIPLY'
00303:     mapping.inputs[1].default_value = (
00304:         (24, 1.4, 5) if kind == "wood" else (1, 1, 1))
00305:     links.new(coord.outputs["Generated"], mapping.inputs[0])
00306:     links.new(mapping.outputs[0], tex.inputs["Vector"])
00307:     ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00308:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .18
00309:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .82
00310:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (
00311:         tuple(v * .72 for v in base[:3]) + (1,))
00312:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (
00313:         tuple(min(v * 1.13, 1) for v in base[:3]) + (1,))
00314:     links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"], ramp.inputs[0])
00315:     links.new(ramp.outputs[0], bsdf.inputs["Base Color"])
00316:     bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00317:     bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .19
00318:     bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .035 if kind == "wood" else .018
00319:     links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"], bump.inputs["Height"])
00320:     links.new(bump.outputs[0], bsdf.inputs["Normal"])
00321:     bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .83
00322: 
00323: 
00324: for mat in list(bpy.data.materials):
00325:     mat.use_nodes = True
00326:     bsdf = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00327:     if bsdf is None:
00328:         continue
00329:     r, g, b, _ = bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].default_value
00330:     if r > g * 1.20 and g > b * 1.18:
00331:         textured_material(mat, "wood")
00332:     elif .30 < r < .77 and .29 < g < .78 and b < .60:
00333:         textured_material(mat, "plaster")
00334:     elif max(r, g, b) < .19:
00335:         bsdf.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = .55
00336:         bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .36
00337:     else:
00338:         bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .84
